0018820: /// 检查一段地址是否完整位于 /proc/self/maps 的可读区间。
0018821: fn is_readable_range(addr: usize, len: usize) -> bool {
0018822:     if addr < 0x10000 || len == 0 {
0018823:         return false;
0018824:     }
0018825:     let end = match addr.checked_add(len) {
0018826:         Some(v) => v,
0018827:         None => return false,
0018828:     };
0018829:     let maps = match std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/maps") {
0018830:         Ok(v) => v,
0018831:         Err(_) => return false,
0018832:     };
0018833:     for line in maps.lines() {
0018834:         let mut parts = line.split_whitespace();
0018835:         let range = match parts.next() {
0018836:             Some(v) => v,
0018837:             None => continue,
0018838:         };
0018839:         let perms = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
0018840:         if !perms.starts_with('r') {
0018841:             continue;
0018842:         }
0018843:         let (start_text, end_text) = match range.split_once('-') {
0018844:             Some(v) => v,
0018845:             None => continue,
0018846:         };
0018847:         let start = match usize::from_str_radix(start_text, 16) {
0018848:             Ok(v) => v,
0018849:             Err(_) => continue,
0018850:         };
0018851:         let map_end = match usize::from_str_radix(end_text, 16) {
0018852:             Ok(v) => v,
0018853:             Err(_) => continue,
0018854:         };
0018855:         if addr >= start && end <= map_end {
0018856:             return true;
0018857:         }
0018858:     }
0018859:     false
0018860: }

0018862: /// 输出诊断对象的原始十六进制内存。地址不可读时返回空字符串。
0018863: unsafe fn debug_hex(addr: *const u8, len: usize) -> String {
0018864:     if addr.is_null() || !is_readable_range(addr as usize, len) {
0018865:         return String::new();
0018866:     }
0018867:     let mut result = String::with_capacity(len * 2);
0018868:     for index in 0..len {
0018869:         let value = std::ptr::read_unaligned(addr.add(index));
0018870:         result.push_str(&format!("{:02x}", value));
0018871:     }
0018872:     result
0018873: }

0022201: /// 辅助函数：IL2CPP类型枚举转可读名称
0022202: fn type_enum_to_name(te: u8) -> String {
0022203:     match te {
0022204:         1 => "void".to_string(),
0022205:         2 => "boolean".to_string(),
0022206:         3 => "char".to_string(),
0022207:         4 => "i1".to_string(),
0022208:         5 => "u1".to_string(),
0022209:         6 => "i2".to_string(),
0022210:         7 => "u2".to_string(),
0022211:         8 => "i4".to_string(),
0022212:         9 => "u4".to_string(),
0022213:         10 => "i8".to_string(),
0022214:         11 => "u8".to_string(),
0022215:         12 => "r4".to_string(),
0022216:         13 => "r8".to_string(),
0022217:         14 => "string".to_string(),
0022218:         17 => "ptr".to_string(),
0022219:         18 => "byref".to_string(),
0022220:         21 => "valuetype".to_string(),
0022221:         22 => "class".to_string(),
0022222:         24 => "array".to_string(),
0022223:         25 => "genericinst".to_string(),
0022224:         28 => "cmplx".to_string(),
0022225:         29 => "fnptr".to_string(),
0022226:         30 => "object".to_string(),
0022227:         _ => format!("type_{}", te),
0022228:     }
0022229: }

0022247: /// v3.22.89: /il2cpp/search_methods?keyword=X — 跨类搜索方法名
0022248: /// 遍历所有IL2CPP类的方法表，按方法名关键词过滤，返回匹配的类名+方法名
0022249: /// 用于定位やる気系数等散落在各类中的计算方法
0022250: unsafe fn il2cpp_search_methods(keyword: &str, letter: &str) -> String {
0022251:     if keyword.is_empty() {
0022252:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?keyword= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0022253:     }
0022254:     let image = get_image();
0022255:     if image.is_null() {
0022256:         return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string();
0022257:     }
0022258: 
0022259:     // 解析IL2CPP API函数指针
0022260:     let get_count_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_image_get_class_count");
0022261:     let get_class_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_image_get_class");
0022262:     if get_count_fn.is_null() || get_class_fn.is_null() {
0022263:         return r#"{"error":"class_enum_api_not_found"}"#.to_string();
0022264:     }
0022265:     let get_count: FnImageGetClassCount = std::mem::transmute(get_count_fn);
0022266:     let get_class: FnImageGetClass = std::mem::transmute(get_class_fn);
0022267: 
0022268:     // 方法遍历API
0022269:     let get_methods_fn: Option<
0022270:         unsafe extern "C" fn(*mut c_void, *mut *mut c_void) -> *const c_void,
0022271:     > = {
0022272:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_methods");
0022273:         if p.is_null() {
0022274:             None
0022275:         } else {
0022276:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022277:         }
0022278:     };
0022279:     let method_get_name_fn: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn(*const c_void) -> *const c_char> = {
0022280:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_name");
0022281:         if p.is_null() {
0022282:             None
0022283:         } else {
0022284:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022285:         }
0022286:     };
0022287:     let method_get_param_count_fn: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn(*const c_void) -> u32> = {
0022288:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_param_count");
0022289:         if p.is_null() {
0022290:             None
0022291:         } else {
0022292:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022293:         }
0022294:     };
0022295:     let method_get_return_type_fn: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn(*const c_void) -> *const c_void> = {
0022296:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_return_type");
0022297:         if p.is_null() {
0022298:             None
0022299:         } else {
0022300:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022301:         }
0022302:     };
0022303:     let method_get_flags_fn: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn(*const c_void, *mut u32) -> u32> = {
0022304:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_flags");
0022305:         if p.is_null() {
0022306:             None
0022307:         } else {
0022308:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022309:         }
0022310:     };
0022311: 
0022312:     if get_methods_fn.is_none() || method_get_name_fn.is_none() {
0022313:         return r#"{"error":"method_enum_api_not_found"}"#.to_string();
0022314:     }
0022315: 
0022316:     // 类名/命名空间读取
0022317:     let get_name_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_name");
0022318:     let get_namespace_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_namespace");
0022319: 
0022320:     let total = get_count(image);
0022321:     let search_lower = keyword.to_lowercase();
0022322:     let mut matched_methods: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
0022323:     let mut classes_with_hits: i32 = 0;
0022324:     let max_results = 200; // 限制最多200个方法结果
0022325: 
0022326:     for i in 0..total {
0022327:         let cls = get_class(image, i);
0022328:         if cls.is_null() {
0022329:             continue;
0022330:         }
0022331: 
0022332:         // 读取类名
0022333:         let class_name = if !get_name_fn.is_null() {
0022334:             let name_fn: FnClassGetName = std::mem::transmute(get_name_fn);
0022335:             let cstr = name_fn(cls);
0022336:             if cstr.is_null() {
0022337:                 continue;
0022338:             }
0022339:             std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(cstr)
0022340:                 .to_string_lossy()
0022341:                 .into_owned()
0022342:         } else {
0022343:             continue;
0022344:         };
0022345: 
0022346:         // 读取命名空间
0022347:         let namespace = if !get_namespace_fn.is_null() {
0022348:             let ns_fn: FnClassGetName = std::mem::transmute(get_namespace_fn);
0022349:             let cstr = ns_fn(cls);
0022350:             if cstr.is_null() {
0022351:                 String::new()
0022352:             } else {
0022353:                 std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(cstr)
0022354:                     .to_string_lossy()
0022355:                     .into_owned()
0022356:             }
0022357:         } else {
0022358:             String::new()
0022359:         };
0022360: 
0022361:         // 按类名首字母过滤（letter参数，A-Z分组下载对策）
0022362:         if !letter.is_empty() {
----- lines 22097..22366 -----
0022097:     // il2cpp_method_get_class 获取方法所属类
0022098:     let method_get_class_fn: Option<unsafe extern "C" fn(*const c_void) -> *mut c_void> = {
0022099:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_class");
0022100:         if p.is_null() {
0022101:             None
0022102:         } else {
0022103:             Some(std::mem::transmute(p))
0022104:         }
0022105:     };
0022106: 
0022107:     if get_methods_fn.is_none() || method_get_name_fn.is_none() {
0022108:         return format!(
0022109:             r#"{{"ok":true,"requested":"{}","found":"{}","method_count":0,"methods":[],"error":"missing_il2cpp_api"}}"#,
0022110:             class_name, real_name
0022111:         );
0022112:     }
0022113: 
0022114:     let mut methods = Vec::new();
0022115:     let mut iter: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0022116:     loop {
0022117:         let method_info = get_methods_fn.unwrap()(class, &mut iter);
0022118:         if method_info.is_null() {
0022119:             break;
0022120:         }
0022121: 
0022122:         let method_name = {
0022123:             let name_ptr = method_get_name_fn.unwrap()(method_info);
0022124:             if name_ptr.is_null() {
0022125:                 "(null)".to_string()
0022126:             } else {
0022127:                 let cstr = CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr);
0022128:                 cstr.to_string_lossy().into_owned()
0022129:             }
0022130:         };
0022131: 
0022132:         // 跳过.ctor和.cctor（构造函数）
0022133:         if method_name.starts_with('.') {
0022134:             continue;
0022135:         }
0022136: 
0022137:         let param_count = method_get_param_count_fn
0022138:             .map(|f| f(method_info))
0022139:             .unwrap_or(0);
0022140: 
0022141:         // 判断是否是静态方法: METHOD_ATTRIBUTE_STATIC = 0x0010
0022142:         let is_static = method_get_flags_fn
0022143:             .map(|f| {
0022144:                 let mut iflags: u32 = 0;
0022145:                 let flags = f(method_info, &mut iflags);
0022146:                 (flags & 0x0010) != 0
0022147:             })
0022148:             .unwrap_or(false);
0022149: 
0022150:         // Preserve the coarse enum for compatibility and add the exact IL2CPP type name.
0022151:         let return_type_ptr = method_get_return_type_fn
0022152:             .map(|f| f(method_info))
0022153:             .unwrap_or(ptr::null());
0022154:         let return_type_str = if return_type_ptr.is_null() {
0022155:             "void".to_string()
0022156:         } else {
0022157:             type_enum_to_name(il2cpp_type_get_type_enum(return_type_ptr))
0022158:         };
0022159:         let return_type_name = if return_type_ptr.is_null() {
0022160:             "void".to_string()
0022161:         } else {
0022162:             type_get_name_fn
0022163:                 .and_then(|f| {
0022164:                     let p = f(return_type_ptr);
0022165:                     if p.is_null() {
0022166:                         None
0022167:                     } else {
0022168:                         Some(CStr::from_ptr(p).to_string_lossy().into_owned())
0022169:                     }
0022170:                 })
0022171:                 .unwrap_or_else(|| return_type_str.clone())
0022172:         };
0022173: 
0022174:         // 检查是否是本类定义的方法（不是继承的）
0022175:         let is_own_method = method_get_class_fn
0022176:             .map(|f| {
0022177:                 let declaring_class = f(method_info);
0022178:                 declaring_class == class
0022179:             })
0022180:             .unwrap_or(true);
0022181: 
0022182:         methods.push(format!(
0022183:             r#"{{"name":"{}","params":{},"return_type":"{}","return_type_name":"{}","static":{},"own":{}}}"#,
0022184:             json_escape(&method_name),
0022185:             param_count,
0022186:             return_type_str,
0022187:             json_escape(&return_type_name),
0022188:             is_static,
0022189:             is_own_method
0022190:         ));
0022191:     }
0022192:     format!(
0022193:         r#"{{"ok":true,"requested":"{}","found":"{}","method_count":{},"methods":[{}]}}"#,
0022194:         class_name,
0022195:         real_name,
0022196:         methods.len(),
0022197:         methods.join(",")
0022198:     )
0022199: }
0022200: 
0022201: /// 辅助函数：IL2CPP类型枚举转可读名称
0022202: fn type_enum_to_name(te: u8) -> String {
0022203:     match te {
0022204:         1 => "void".to_string(),
0022205:         2 => "boolean".to_string(),
0022206:         3 => "char".to_string(),
0022207:         4 => "i1".to_string(),
0022208:         5 => "u1".to_string(),
0022209:         6 => "i2".to_string(),
0022210:         7 => "u2".to_string(),
0022211:         8 => "i4".to_string(),
0022212:         9 => "u4".to_string(),
0022213:         10 => "i8".to_string(),
0022214:         11 => "u8".to_string(),
0022215:         12 => "r4".to_string(),
0022216:         13 => "r8".to_string(),
0022217:         14 => "string".to_string(),
0022218:         17 => "ptr".to_string(),
0022219:         18 => "byref".to_string(),
0022220:         21 => "valuetype".to_string(),
0022221:         22 => "class".to_string(),
0022222:         24 => "array".to_string(),
0022223:         25 => "genericinst".to_string(),
0022224:         28 => "cmplx".to_string(),
0022225:         29 => "fnptr".to_string(),
0022226:         30 => "object".to_string(),
0022227:         _ => format!("type_{}", te),
0022228:     }
0022229: }

0009275: /// Convert PascalCase to snake_case for cache key matching
0009276: fn to_snake_case(name: &str) -> String {
0009277:     let mut result = String::new();
0009278:     for (i, c) in name.chars().enumerate() {
0009279:         if c.is_uppercase() && i > 0 {
0009280:             result.push('_');
0009281:         }
0009282:         result.extend(c.to_lowercase());
0009283:     }
0009284:     result
0009285: }

0009287: /// Pre-cache ALL field offsets for a class (including parent classes)
0009288: /// Called on game thread — safe to use IL2CPP API
0009289: unsafe fn precache_all_fields(class: *mut c_void) {
0009290:     if class.is_null() {
0009291:         return;
0009292:     }
0009293:     let get_fields_fn: Option<FnClassGetFields> = {
0009294:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_fields");
0009295:         if p.is_null() {
0009296:             None
0009297:         } else {
0009298:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetFields>(p))
0009299:         }
0009300:     };
0009301:     let get_parent_fn: Option<FnClassGetParent> = {
0009302:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_parent");
0009303:         if p.is_null() {
0009304:             None
0009305:         } else {
0009306:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetParent>(p))
0009307:         }
0009308:     };
0009309:     if get_fields_fn.is_none() {
0009310:         return;
0009311:     }
0009312: 
0009313:     let mut current_class = class;
0009314:     let mut depth = 0;
0009315:     loop {
0009316:         if current_class.is_null() || depth > 10 {
0009317:             break;
0009318:         }
0009319:         let mut iter: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0009320:         loop {
0009321:             let field_info = get_fields_fn.unwrap()(current_class, &mut iter);
0009322:             if field_info.is_null() {
0009323:                 break;
0009324:             }
0009325:             if !(*field_info).name.is_null() {
0009326:                 let s = std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr((*field_info).name);
0009327:                 let fname = s.to_string_lossy().to_string();
0009328:                 let offset = (*field_info).offset;
0009329:                 // Extract property name from <PropName>k__BackingField

0009331:                     if let Some(end) = fname.find('>') {
0009332:                         &fname[1..end]
0009333:                     } else {
0009334:                         &fname
0009335:                     }
0009336:                 } else {
0009337:                     &fname
0009338:                 };
0009339:                 // Store multiple cache keys for robust lookup
0009340:                 let keys = [
0009341:                     format!("{:p}_{}", class, prop_name),
0009342:                     format!("{:p}_{}", class, prop_name.to_lowercase()),
0009343:                     format!("{:p}_{}", class, to_snake_case(prop_name)),
0009344:                 ];
0009345:                 if let Ok(mut guard) = FIELD_OFFSET_CACHE.lock() {
0009346:                     if guard.is_none() {

0022179:             })

0009007:             if ptr_addr == 0 {
0009008:                 return r#"{"error":"invalid_ptr_addr"}"#.to_string();

0009011:                 let p = std::ptr::read::<u64>(ptr_addr as *const u64);

0009013:             }

0016706:             let parent = get_parent(current_class);
0016707:             if parent.is_null() || parent == current_class {
0016708:                 break;
0016709:             }
0016710:             current_class = parent;

0016712:             break;
0016713:         }
0016714:         depth += 1;
0016715:     }
0016716:     result

0016791: /// /il2cpp/invoke_static?class=X&method=Y&p0=..&n=2
0016792: /// v3.27: 基于 il2cpp_runtime_invoke 的安全 static 方法调用
0016793: /// 不用裸函数指针, 不缺 MethodInfo*, 不崩
0016794: unsafe fn il2cpp_invoke_static_method(
0016795:     class_name: &str,
0016796:     method_name: &str,
0016797:     p0: i64,
0016798:     p1: i64,
0016799:     p2: i64,
0016800:     p3: i64,
0016801:     p4: i64,
0016802:     param_count: i32,
0016803: ) -> String {
0016804:     if class_name.is_empty() || method_name.is_empty() {
0016805:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?class= or ?method="}"#.to_string();
0016806:     }
0016807:     if !(0..=5).contains(&param_count) {
0016808:         return r#"{"error":"param_count_out_of_range"}"#.to_string();
0016809:     }
0016810:     let image = get_image();
0016811:     if image.is_null() {
0016812:         return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string();
0016813:     }
0016814:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016815:     if class.is_null() {
0016816:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016817:     }
0016818: 
0016819:     let get_method_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name");
0016820:     let invoke_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_runtime_invoke");
0016821:     if get_method_ptr.is_null() || invoke_ptr.is_null() {
0016822:         return r#"{"error":"runtime_symbols_not_found"}"#.to_string();
0016823:     }
0016824:     let get_method: FnClassGetMethodFromName = std::mem::transmute(get_method_ptr);
0016825:     let invoke: FnRuntimeInvoke = std::mem::transmute(invoke_ptr);
0016826:     let method_info = get_method(class, to_cstr(method_name).as_ptr(), param_count);
0016827:     if method_info.is_null() {
0016828:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"method_not_found","class":"{}","method":"{}","params":{}}}"#, class_name, method_name, param_count);
0016829:     }
0016830: 
0016831:     // This endpoint accepts i32-compatible value parameters only.
0016832:     // runtime_invoke wants pointers to raw values, not boxed Int32 objects.
0016833:     let mut raw_args = [p0 as i32, p1 as i32, p2 as i32, p3 as i32, p4 as i32];
0016834:     let mut argv: [*mut c_void; 5] = [ptr::null_mut(); 5];
0016835:     for index in 0..param_count as usize {
0016836:         argv[index] = &mut raw_args[index] as *mut i32 as *mut c_void;
0016837:     }
0016838:     let argv_ptr = if param_count == 0 {
0016839:         ptr::null_mut()
0016840:     } else {
0016841:         argv.as_mut_ptr()
0016842:     };
0016843:     let mut exc: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0016844:     let result = invoke(method_info, ptr::null_mut(), argv_ptr, &mut exc);
0016845:     if !exc.is_null() {
0016846:         return format!(r#"{{"ok":false,"class":"{}","method":"{}","error":"exception","exc_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, exc as usize);
0016847:     }
0016848:     format!(r#"{{"ok":true,"class":"{}","method":"{}","params":{},"result_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, param_count, result as usize)
0016849: }

0016791: /// /il2cpp/invoke_static?class=X&method=Y&p0=..&n=2
0016792: /// v3.27: 基于 il2cpp_runtime_invoke 的安全 static 方法调用
0016793: /// 不用裸函数指针, 不缺 MethodInfo*, 不崩
0016794: unsafe fn il2cpp_invoke_static_method(
0016795:     class_name: &str,
0016796:     method_name: &str,
0016797:     p0: i64,
0016798:     p1: i64,
0016799:     p2: i64,
0016800:     p3: i64,
0016801:     p4: i64,
0016802:     param_count: i32,
0016803: ) -> String {
0016804:     if class_name.is_empty() || method_name.is_empty() {
0016805:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?class= or ?method="}"#.to_string();
0016806:     }
0016807:     if !(0..=5).contains(&param_count) {
0016808:         return r#"{"error":"param_count_out_of_range"}"#.to_string();
0016809:     }
0016810:     let image = get_image();
0016811:     if image.is_null() {
0016812:         return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string();
0016813:     }
0016814:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016815:     if class.is_null() {
0016816:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016817:     }
0016818: 
0016819:     let get_method_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name");
0016820:     let invoke_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_runtime_invoke");
0016821:     if get_method_ptr.is_null() || invoke_ptr.is_null() {
0016822:         return r#"{"error":"runtime_symbols_not_found"}"#.to_string();
0016823:     }
0016824:     let get_method: FnClassGetMethodFromName = std::mem::transmute(get_method_ptr);
0016825:     let invoke: FnRuntimeInvoke = std::mem::transmute(invoke_ptr);
0016826:     let method_info = get_method(class, to_cstr(method_name).as_ptr(), param_count);
0016827:     if method_info.is_null() {
0016828:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"method_not_found","class":"{}","method":"{}","params":{}}}"#, class_name, method_name, param_count);
0016829:     }
0016830: 
0016831:     // This endpoint accepts i32-compatible value parameters only.
0016832:     // runtime_invoke wants pointers to raw values, not boxed Int32 objects.
0016833:     let mut raw_args = [p0 as i32, p1 as i32, p2 as i32, p3 as i32, p4 as i32];
0016834:     let mut argv: [*mut c_void; 5] = [ptr::null_mut(); 5];
0016835:     for index in 0..param_count as usize {
0016836:         argv[index] = &mut raw_args[index] as *mut i32 as *mut c_void;
0016837:     }
0016838:     let argv_ptr = if param_count == 0 {
0016839:         ptr::null_mut()
0016840:     } else {
0016841:         argv.as_mut_ptr()
0016842:     };
0016843:     let mut exc: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0016844:     let result = invoke(method_info, ptr::null_mut(), argv_ptr, &mut exc);
0016845:     if !exc.is_null() {
0016846:         return format!(r#"{{"ok":false,"class":"{}","method":"{}","error":"exception","exc_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, exc as usize);
0016847:     }
0016848:     format!(r#"{{"ok":true,"class":"{}","method":"{}","params":{},"result_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, param_count, result as usize)
0016849: }

0016920: /// /il2cpp/call_static?class=X&method=Y&p0=..&p1=..&p2=..&p3=..&p4=..&n=5
0016921: /// 调用static方法，无需singleton实例
0016922: /// 支持最多5个int参数 + 第5个参数可作为指针(null=0)
0016923: unsafe fn il2cpp_call_static_method(
0016924:     class_name: &str,
0016925:     method_name: &str,
0016926:     p0: i64,
0016927:     p1: i64,
0016928:     p2: i64,
0016929:     p3: i64,
0016930:     p4: i64,
0016931:     param_count: i32,
0016932: ) -> String {
0016933:     if class_name.is_empty() || method_name.is_empty() {
0016934:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?class= or ?method= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0016935:     }
0016936:     if API.is_null() {
0016937:         return r#"{"error":"api_null"}"#.to_string();
0016938:     }
0016939:     let api = &*API;
0016940:     let image = match get_image() {
0016941:         img if !img.is_null() => img,
0016942:         _ => return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string(),
0016943:     };
0016944:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016945:     if class.is_null() {
0016946:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016947:     }
0016948:     // 用 il2cpp_get_method_addr_fn 找方法地址
0016949:     let get_method_addr = match api.il2cpp_get_method_addr_fn {
0016950:         Some(f) => f,
0016951:         None => return r#"{"error":"no_method_addr_fn"}"#.to_string(),
0016952:     };
0016953:     // 尝试不同的参数数量找到方法
0016954:     let mut method_addr: usize = 0;
0016955:     for &n in &[param_count, 5, 4, 6, 3, 2, 1, 0] {
0016956:         method_addr = get_method_addr(class as usize, to_cstr(method_name).as_ptr(), n);
0016957:         if method_addr != 0 { break; }
0016958:     }
0016959:     if method_addr == 0 {
0016960:         return format!(
0016961:             r#"{{"error":"method_not_found","class":"{}","method":"{}"}}"#,
0016962:             class_name, method_name
0016963:         );
0016964:     }
0016965:     // 调用 static 方法
0016966:     // ARM64 ABI: 参数 x0-x4, static 方法无 this 指针
0016967:     // 参数1-4 是 int (w寄存器), 参数5 是指针 (x寄存器)
0016968:     // p4=0 表示 null delegate
0016969:     let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
0016970:         match param_count {
0016971:             0 => {
0016972:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn() -> *mut c_void;
0016973:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016974:                 f()
0016975:             }
0016976:             1 => {
0016977:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016978:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016979:                 f(p0)
0016980:             }
0016981:             2 => {
0016982:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016983:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016984:                 f(p0, p1)
0016985:             }
0016986:             3 => {
0016987:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016988:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016989:                 f(p0, p1, p2)
0016990:             }
0016991:             4 => {
0016992:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016993:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016994:                 f(p0, p1, p2, p3)
0016995:             }
0016996:             _ => {
0016997:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016998:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016999:                 f(p0, p1, p2, p3, p4)
0017000:             }
0017001:         }
0017002:     }));
0017003:     match result {
0017004:         Ok(ret) => {
0017005:             let ret_addr = ret as usize;
0017006:             format!(
0017007:                 r#"{{"ok":true,"class":"{}","method":"{}","method_addr":"0x{:x}","params":[{},{},{},{},{}],"ret":"0x{:x}"}}"#,
0017008:                 class_name, method_name, method_addr,
0017009:                 p0, p1, p2, p3, p4, ret_addr
0017010:             )
0017011:         }
0017012:         Err(_) => format!(
0017013:             r#"{{"ok":false,"class":"{}","method":"{}","error":"panic"}}"#,
0017014:             class_name, method_name
0017015:         ),
0017016:     }
0017017: }

0016853: /// v3.27: 基于 il2cpp_runtime_invoke 的安全实例方法调用
0016854: unsafe fn il2cpp_invoke_instance_method(
0016855:     class_name: &str,
0016856:     method_name: &str,
0016857:     object_addr: &str,
0016858:     p0: i64,
0016859:     p1: i64,
0016860:     p2: i64,
0016861:     p3: i64,
0016862:     p4: i64,
0016863:     param_count: i32,
0016864: ) -> String {
0016865:     if class_name.is_empty() || method_name.is_empty() {
0016866:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?class= or ?method="}"#.to_string();
0016867:     }
0016868:     if !(0..=5).contains(&param_count) {
0016869:         return r#"{"error":"param_count_out_of_range"}"#.to_string();
0016870:     }
0016871:     let obj_addr = usize::from_str_radix(object_addr.trim_start_matches("0x"), 16).unwrap_or(0);
0016872:     if obj_addr == 0 {
0016873:         return r#"{"error":"missing or invalid ?object= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0016874:     }
0016875:     let image = get_image();
0016876:     if image.is_null() {
0016877:         return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string();
0016878:     }
0016879:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016880:     if class.is_null() {
0016881:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016882:     }
0016883: 
0016884:     let get_method_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name");
0016885:     let invoke_ptr = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_runtime_invoke");
0016886:     if get_method_ptr.is_null() || invoke_ptr.is_null() {
0016887:         return r#"{"error":"runtime_symbols_not_found"}"#.to_string();
0016888:     }
0016889:     let get_method: FnClassGetMethodFromName = std::mem::transmute(get_method_ptr);
0016890:     let invoke: FnRuntimeInvoke = std::mem::transmute(invoke_ptr);
0016891:     let method_info = get_method(class, to_cstr(method_name).as_ptr(), param_count);
0016892:     if method_info.is_null() {
0016893:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"method_not_found","class":"{}","method":"{}","params":{}}}"#, class_name, method_name, param_count);
0016894:     }
0016895: 
0016896:     let mut raw_args = [p0 as i32, p1 as i32, p2 as i32, p3 as i32, p4 as i32];
0016897:     let mut argv: [*mut c_void; 5] = [ptr::null_mut(); 5];
0016898:     for index in 0..param_count as usize {
0016899:         argv[index] = &mut raw_args[index] as *mut i32 as *mut c_void;
0016900:     }
0016901:     let argv_ptr = if param_count == 0 {
0016902:         ptr::null_mut()
0016903:     } else {
0016904:         argv.as_mut_ptr()
0016905:     };
0016906:     let mut exc: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0016907:     let result = invoke(
0016908:         method_info,
0016909:         obj_addr as *mut c_void,
0016910:         argv_ptr,
0016911:         &mut exc,
0016912:     );
0016913:     if !exc.is_null() {
0016914:         return format!(r#"{{"ok":false,"class":"{}","method":"{}","error":"exception","exc_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, exc as usize);
0016915:     }
0016916:     format!(r#"{{"ok":true,"class":"{}","method":"{}","object":"0x{:x}","params":{},"result_addr":"0x{:x}"}}"#, class_name, method_name, obj_addr, param_count, result as usize)
0016917: }

0016920: /// /il2cpp/call_static?class=X&method=Y&p0=..&p1=..&p2=..&p3=..&p4=..&n=5
0016921: /// 调用static方法，无需singleton实例
0016922: /// 支持最多5个int参数 + 第5个参数可作为指针(null=0)
0016923: unsafe fn il2cpp_call_static_method(
0016924:     class_name: &str,
0016925:     method_name: &str,
0016926:     p0: i64,
0016927:     p1: i64,
0016928:     p2: i64,
0016929:     p3: i64,
0016930:     p4: i64,
0016931:     param_count: i32,
0016932: ) -> String {
0016933:     if class_name.is_empty() || method_name.is_empty() {
0016934:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?class= or ?method= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0016935:     }
0016936:     if API.is_null() {
0016937:         return r#"{"error":"api_null"}"#.to_string();
0016938:     }
0016939:     let api = &*API;
0016940:     let image = match get_image() {
0016941:         img if !img.is_null() => img,
0016942:         _ => return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string(),
0016943:     };
0016944:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016945:     if class.is_null() {
0016946:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016947:     }
0016948:     // 用 il2cpp_get_method_addr_fn 找方法地址
0016949:     let get_method_addr = match api.il2cpp_get_method_addr_fn {
0016950:         Some(f) => f,
0016951:         None => return r#"{"error":"no_method_addr_fn"}"#.to_string(),
0016952:     };
0016953:     // 尝试不同的参数数量找到方法
0016954:     let mut method_addr: usize = 0;
0016955:     for &n in &[param_count, 5, 4, 6, 3, 2, 1, 0] {
0016956:         method_addr = get_method_addr(class as usize, to_cstr(method_name).as_ptr(), n);
0016957:         if method_addr != 0 { break; }
0016958:     }
0016959:     if method_addr == 0 {
0016960:         return format!(
0016961:             r#"{{"error":"method_not_found","class":"{}","method":"{}"}}"#,
0016962:             class_name, method_name
0016963:         );
0016964:     }
0016965:     // 调用 static 方法
0016966:     // ARM64 ABI: 参数 x0-x4, static 方法无 this 指针
0016967:     // 参数1-4 是 int (w寄存器), 参数5 是指针 (x寄存器)
0016968:     // p4=0 表示 null delegate
0016969:     let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
0016970:         match param_count {
0016971:             0 => {
0016972:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn() -> *mut c_void;
0016973:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016974:                 f()
0016975:             }
0016976:             1 => {
0016977:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016978:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016979:                 f(p0)
0016980:             }
0016981:             2 => {
0016982:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016983:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016984:                 f(p0, p1)
0016985:             }
0016986:             3 => {
0016987:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016988:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016989:                 f(p0, p1, p2)
0016990:             }
0016991:             4 => {
0016992:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016993:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016994:                 f(p0, p1, p2, p3)
0016995:             }
0016996:             _ => {
0016997:                 type F = unsafe extern "C" fn(i64, i64, i64, i64, i64) -> *mut c_void;
0016998:                 let f: F = std::mem::transmute(method_addr);
0016999:                 f(p0, p1, p2, p3, p4)
0017000:             }
0017001:         }
0017002:     }));
0017003:     match result {
0017004:         Ok(ret) => {
0017005:             let ret_addr = ret as usize;
0017006:             format!(
0017007:                 r#"{{"ok":true,"class":"{}","method":"{}","method_addr":"0x{:x}","params":[{},{},{},{},{}],"ret":"0x{:x}"}}"#,
0017008:                 class_name, method_name, method_addr,
0017009:                 p0, p1, p2, p3, p4, ret_addr
0017010:             )
0017011:         }
0017012:         Err(_) => format!(
0017013:             r#"{{"ok":false,"class":"{}","method":"{}","error":"panic"}}"#,
0017014:             class_name, method_name
0017015:         ),
0017016:     }
0017017: }

0003144: unsafe fn enumerate_class_methods(class: *mut c_void) -> String {
0003145:     if class.is_null() || API.is_null() {
0003146:         return r#"{"error":"null_class"}"#.to_string();
0003147:     }
0003148: 
0003149:     let get_methods_fn: Option<FnClassGetMethods> = {
0003150:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_methods");
0003151:         if p.is_null() {
0003152:             None
0003153:         } else {
0003154:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetMethods>(p))
0003155:         }
0003156:     };
0003157:     let get_method_name_fn: Option<FnMethodGetName> = {
0003158:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_name");
0003159:         if p.is_null() {
0003160:             None
0003161:         } else {
0003162:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnMethodGetName>(p))
0003163:         }
0003164:     };
0003165:     let get_parent_fn: Option<FnClassGetParent> = {
0003166:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_parent");
0003167:         if p.is_null() {
0003168:             None
0003169:         } else {
0003170:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetParent>(p))
0003171:         }
0003172:     };
0003173:     let get_class_name_fn: Option<FnClassGetName> = {
0003174:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_name");
0003175:         if p.is_null() {
0003176:             None
0003177:         } else {
0003178:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetName>(p))
0003179:         }
0003180:     };
0003181: 
0003182:     if get_methods_fn.is_none() {
0003183:         return r#"{"error":"no_il2cpp_class_get_methods"}"#.to_string();
0003184:     }
0003185: 
0003186:     let mut all_methods: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
0003187:     let mut current_class = class;
0003188:     let mut depth = 0;
0003189:     let max_methods = 500;
0003190: 
0003191:     loop {
0003192:         if current_class.is_null() || depth > 5 {
0003193:             break;
0003194:         }
0003195:         if all_methods.len() >= max_methods {
0003196:             break;
0003197:         }
0003198: 
0003199:         let class_name = if let Some(ref get_name) = get_class_name_fn {
0003200:             let name_ptr = get_name(current_class);
0003201:             if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0003202:                 let s = std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char);
0003203:                 s.to_string_lossy().to_string()
0003204:             } else {
0003205:                 format!("depth{}", depth)
0003206:             }
0003207:         } else {
0003208:             format!("depth{}", depth)
0003209:         };
0003210: 
0003211:         let mut iter: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0003212:         loop {
0003213:             if all_methods.len() >= max_methods {
0003214:                 break;
0003215:             }
0003216:             let method_info = get_methods_fn.unwrap()(current_class, &mut iter);
0003217:             if method_info.is_null() {
0003218:                 break;
0003219:             }
0003220: 
0003221:             let method_name = if let Some(ref get_name) = get_method_name_fn {
0003222:                 let name_ptr = get_name(method_info);
0003223:                 if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0003224:                     let s = std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char);
0003225:                     s.to_string_lossy().to_string()
0003226:                 } else {
0003227:                     String::from("?")
0003228:                 }
0003229:             } else {
0003230:                 String::from("?")
0003231:             };
0003232: 
0003233:             all_methods.push(format!(
0003234:                 r#"{{"name":"{}","class":"{}"}}"#,
0003235:                 method_name, class_name
0003236:             ));
0003237:         }
0003238: 
0003239:         if let Some(ref get_parent) = get_parent_fn {
0003240:             let parent = get_parent(current_class);
0003241:             if parent.is_null() || parent == current_class {
0003242:                 break;
0003243:             }
0003244:             current_class = parent;
0003245:         } else {
0003246:             break;
0003247:         }
0003248:         depth += 1;
0003249:     }
0003250: 
0003251:     format!(
0003252:         r#"{{"total":{},"methods":[{}]}}"#,
0003253:         all_methods.len(),
0003254:         all_methods.join(",")

0003262: unsafe fn find_method_in_all_classes(method_name: &str) -> String {

0003275:             None
0003276:         } else {
0003277:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetMethodFromName>(p))
0003278:         }
0003279:     };
0003280:

0003282:         return r#"{"error":"no_class_get_method_from_name"}"#.to_string();
0003283:     }

0003286:     let mut found: Vec<String> = Vec::new();

0003289:         let ns_c = to_cstr(ns);

0003294:         }

0003297:             cls.to_string()

0003299:             format!("{}.{}", ns, cls)
0003300:         };
0003301: 
0003302:         let method = get_method_fn.unwrap()(class, method_name_c.as_ptr(), 0);
0003303:         if !method.is_null() {
0003304:             found.push(format!(r#"{{"class":"{}","args":0}}"#, full_name));
0003305:         }
0003306: 
0003307:         let method1 = get_method_fn.unwrap()(class, method_name_c.as_ptr(), 1);
0003308:         if !method1.is_null() && method.is_null() {
0003309:             found.push(format!(r#"{{"class":"{}","args":1}}"#, full_name));
0003310:         }
0003311:     }
0003312: 
0003313:     format!(
0003314:         r#"{{"method":"{}","found":{},"results":[{}]}}"#,
0003315:         method_name,
0003316:         !found.is_empty(),

0013101: fn private_app_root() -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, String> {
0013102:     let bytes = std::fs::read("/proc/self/cmdline").map_err(|_| "cmdline_unavailable")?;
0013103:     let pkg = bytes
0013104:         .split(|&b| b == 0)
0013105:         .find(|s| !s.is_empty())
0013106:         .and_then(|s| std::str::from_utf8(s).ok())
0013107:         .ok_or("package_unknown")?;

0013109:     if pkg.is_empty()
0013110:         || !pkg
0013111:             .chars()
0013112:             .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '.' || c == '_')
0013113:     {
0013114:         return Err("invalid_package_name".to_string());
0013115:     }
0013116:     for root in [
0013117:         format!("/data/user/0/{}", pkg),
0013118:         format!("/data/data/{}", pkg),
0013119:     ] {
0013120:         let path = std::path::PathBuf::from(root);
0013121:         if path.is_dir() {
0013122:             return Ok(path);
0013123:         }
0013124:     }
0013125:     Err("private_app_root_not_found".to_string())
0013126: }

0013128: fn classify_private_file(path: &std::path::Path) -> &'static str {
0013129:     let name = path
0013130:         .file_name()
0013131:         .and_then(|v| v.to_str())
0013132:         .unwrap_or("")
0013133:         .to_ascii_lowercase();
0013134:     if name == "meta" || name.starts_with("meta-") {
0013135:         "resource_index"
0013136:     } else if name.ends_with(".db") || name.ends_with(".sqlite") || name.ends_with(".mdb") {
0013137:         "database"
0013138:     } else if name.ends_with(".xml") {
0013139:         "xml"
0013140:     } else if name.ends_with(".json") {
0013141:         "json"
0013142:     } else if name.ends_with(".log") || name.ends_with(".txt") {
0013143:         "text"
0013144:     } else if name.ends_with(".apk") {
0013145:         "apk"
0013146:     } else if name.ends_with(".so") {
0013147:         "native_library"
0013148:     } else {
0013149:         "binary"
0013150:     }
0013151: }

0013153: /// Build a deterministic, bounded snapshot of private files. `dat` is skipped
0013154: /// because its hash objects are already available through resource_file_dl.
0013155: fn private_file_inventory() -> Result<Vec<PrivateFileEntry>, String> {
0013156:     use std::collections::VecDeque;
0013157:     let root = private_app_root()?;
0013158:     let mut queue = VecDeque::from([(root.clone(), 0usize)]);
0013159:     let mut rows: Vec<(String, String, u64, &'static str)> = Vec::new();
0013160:     let mut visited_dirs = 0usize;
0013161:     while let Some((dir, depth)) = queue.pop_front() {
0013162:         visited_dirs += 1;
0013163:         if visited_dirs > 4096 || rows.len() >= 20000 {
0013164:             break;
0013165:         }
0013166:         let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(&dir) {
0013167:             Ok(v) => v,
0013168:             Err(_) => continue,
0013169:         };
0013170:         for entry in entries.flatten() {
0013171:             let ty = match entry.file_type() {
0013172:                 Ok(v) => v,
0013173:                 Err(_) => continue,
0013174:             };
0013175:             if ty.is_symlink() {
0013176:                 continue;
0013177:             }
0013178:             let path = entry.path();
0013179:             if ty.is_dir() {
0013180:                 if depth < 10 && entry.file_name() != "dat" {
0013181:                     queue.push_back((path, depth + 1));
0013182:                 }
0013183:                 continue;
0013184:             }
0013185:             if !ty.is_file() {
0013186:                 continue;
0013187:             }
0013188:             let relative = path
0013189:                 .strip_prefix(&root)
0013190:                 .unwrap_or(&path)
0013191:                 .to_string_lossy()
0013192:                 .into_owned();
0013193:             let size = entry.metadata().map(|m| m.len()).unwrap_or(0);
0013194:             let kind = classify_private_file(&path);
0013195:             rows.push((relative, path.to_string_lossy().into_owned(), size, kind));
0013196:             if rows.len() >= 20000 {
0013197:                 break;
0013198:             }
0013199:         }
0013200:     }
0013201:     rows.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
----- lines 16343..16612 -----
0016343:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"empty_list","list_size":{}}}"#, list_size);
0016344:     }

0016346: 
0016347:     // 6. Find TrainedCharaData class
0016348:     let tcd_class = find_class_by_short_name(image, "TrainedCharaData");

0016350:         ura_log(1, "/hall: TrainedCharaData class not found");

0016352:     }

0016366:         );

0016368:             continue;

0016374:         let stamina = call_getter_int(tcd_class, elem_ptr, "get_Stamina");

0016380:         let scenario_id = call_getter_obscured_int(tcd_class, elem_ptr, "get_ScenarioId");
0016381:         let fans = call_getter_int(tcd_class, elem_ptr, "get_Fans");
0016382:         let rarity = call_getter_obscured_int(tcd_class, elem_ptr, "get_Rarity");

0016390:             r#"{{"idx":{},"card_id":{},"speed":{},"stamina":{},"power":{},"guts":{},"wiz":{},"rank_score":{},"rank":{},"scenario_id":{},"fans":{},"rarity":{}}}"#,
0016391:             i, card_id, speed, stamina, power, guts, wiz, rank_score, rank, scenario_id, fans, rarity
0016392:         ));

0016400:     format!(

0016457: /// v3.22.51: /debug/dumpclass","/debug/storydata?name=ClassName — Dump all fields of any IL2CPP class
0016458: /// Uses il2cpp_class_get_fields (metadata only, no runtime_invoke)
0016459: unsafe fn debug_dumpclass(class_name: &str) -> String {
0016460:     if class_name.is_empty() {
0016461:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?name= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0016462:     }
0016463:     if API.is_null() {
0016464:         return r#"{"error":"api_null"}"#.to_string();
0016465:     }
0016466:     let image = match get_image() {
0016467:         img if !img.is_null() => img,
0016468:         _ => return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string(),
0016469:     };
0016470: 
0016471:     // Try to find class by short name
0016472:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016473:     if class.is_null() {
0016474:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016475:     }
0016476: 
0016477:     // Get class name from IL2CPP
0016478:     let get_name_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_name");
0016479:     let real_name = if !get_name_fn.is_null() {
0016480:         let get_name: FnClassGetName = std::mem::transmute(get_name_fn);
0016481:         let name_ptr = get_name(class);
0016482:         if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0016483:             std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char)
0016484:                 .to_string_lossy()
0016485:                 .to_string()
0016486:         } else {
0016487:             String::new()
0016488:         }
0016489:     } else {
0016490:         String::new()
0016491:     };
0016492: 
0016493:     // Enumerate all fields (including parent classes)
0016494:     let fields_json = enumerate_class_fields(class);
0016495: 
0016496:     // Also enumerate methods (for debugging)
0016497:     let methods_json = enumerate_class_methods(class);
0016498: 
0016499:     format!(
0016500:         r#"{{"requested":"{}","found":"{}","fields":{},"methods":{}}}"#,
0016501:         class_name, real_name, fields_json, methods_json
0016502:     )
0016503: }

0016505: // v3.22.89: IL2CPP运行时值dump — 新增端点

0016459: unsafe fn debug_dumpclass(class_name: &str) -> String {

0016461:         return r#"{"error":"missing ?name= parameter"}"#.to_string();
0016462:     }
0016463:     if API.is_null() {
0016464:         return r#"{"error":"api_null"}"#.to_string();
0016465:     }
0016466:     let image = match get_image() {
0016467:         img if !img.is_null() => img,
0016468:         _ => return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string(),
0016469:     };
0016470: 
0016471:     // Try to find class by short name
0016472:     let class = find_class_by_short_name(image, class_name);
0016473:     if class.is_null() {
0016474:         return format!(r#"{{"error":"class_not_found","name":"{}"}}"#, class_name);
0016475:     }
0016476: 
0016477:     // Get class name from IL2CPP
0016478:     let get_name_fn = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_name");
0016479:     let real_name = if !get_name_fn.is_null() {
0016480:         let get_name: FnClassGetName = std::mem::transmute(get_name_fn);
0016481:         let name_ptr = get_name(class);
0016482:         if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0016483:             std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char)
0016484:                 .to_string_lossy()
0016485:                 .to_string()
0016486:         } else {
0016487:             String::new()
0016488:         }
0016489:     } else {
0016490:         String::new()
0016491:     };
0016492: 
0016493:     // Enumerate all fields (including parent classes)
0016494:     let fields_json = enumerate_class_fields(class);
0016495: 
0016496:     // Also enumerate methods (for debugging)
0016497:     let methods_json = enumerate_class_methods(class);
0016498: 
0016499:     format!(
0016500:         r#"{{"requested":"{}","found":"{}","fields":{},"methods":{}}}"#,
0016501:         class_name, real_name, fields_json, methods_json
0016502:     )
0016503: }

0001221: unsafe fn call_getter_on_instance(
0001222:     class: *mut c_void,
0001223:     instance: *const c_void,
0001224:     method_name: &str,
0001225: ) -> *mut c_void {
0001226:     if class.is_null() || instance.is_null() {
0001227:         return ptr::null_mut();
0001228:     }
0001229:     let get_method_fn: Option<FnClassGetMethodFromName> = {
0001230:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name");
0001231:         if p.is_null() {
0001232:             None
0001233:         } else {
0001234:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetMethodFromName>(p))
0001235:         }
0001236:     };
0001237:     let invoke_fn: Option<FnRuntimeInvoke> = {
0001238:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_runtime_invoke");
0001239:         if p.is_null() {
0001240:             None
0001241:         } else {
0001242:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnRuntimeInvoke>(p))
0001243:         }
0001244:     };
0001245: 
0001246:     if get_method_fn.is_none() || invoke_fn.is_none() {
0001247:         return ptr::null_mut();
0001248:     }
0001249: 
0001250:     let method_name_c = to_cstr(method_name);
0001251:     let method_info = get_method_fn.unwrap()(class, method_name_c.as_ptr(), 0);
0001252:     if method_info.is_null() {
0001253:         ura_log(4, &format!("call_getter: '{}' not found", method_name));
0001254:         return ptr::null_mut();
0001255:     }
0001256: 
0001257:     let mut exc: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0001258:     let result = invoke_fn.unwrap()(
0001259:         method_info,
0001260:         instance as *mut c_void,
0001261:         ptr::null_mut(),
0001262:         &mut exc,
0001263:     );
0001264: 
0001265:     if !exc.is_null() {
0001266:         ura_log(
0001267:             1,
0001268:             &format!("call_getter: '{}' threw exception", method_name),
0001269:         );
0001270:         return ptr::null_mut();

0001274: }

0003144: unsafe fn enumerate_class_methods(class: *mut c_void) -> String {
0003145:     if class.is_null() || API.is_null() {
0003146:         return r#"{"error":"null_class"}"#.to_string();
0003147:     }
0003148: 
0003149:     let get_methods_fn: Option<FnClassGetMethods> = {
0003150:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_methods");
0003151:         if p.is_null() {
0003152:             None
0003153:         } else {
0003154:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetMethods>(p))
0003155:         }
0003156:     };
0003157:     let get_method_name_fn: Option<FnMethodGetName> = {
0003158:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_method_get_name");
0003159:         if p.is_null() {
0003160:             None
0003161:         } else {
0003162:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnMethodGetName>(p))
0003163:         }
0003164:     };
0003165:     let get_parent_fn: Option<FnClassGetParent> = {
0003166:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_parent");
0003167:         if p.is_null() {
0003168:             None
0003169:         } else {
0003170:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetParent>(p))
0003171:         }
0003172:     };
0003173:     let get_class_name_fn: Option<FnClassGetName> = {
0003174:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_name");
0003175:         if p.is_null() {
0003176:             None
0003177:         } else {
0003178:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetName>(p))
0003179:         }
0003180:     };
0003181: 
0003182:     if get_methods_fn.is_none() {
0003183:         return r#"{"error":"no_il2cpp_class_get_methods"}"#.to_string();
0003184:     }
0003185: 
0003186:     let mut all_methods: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
0003187:     let mut current_class = class;
0003188:     let mut depth = 0;
0003189:     let max_methods = 500;
0003190: 
0003191:     loop {
0003192:         if current_class.is_null() || depth > 5 {
0003193:             break;
0003194:         }
0003195:         if all_methods.len() >= max_methods {
0003196:             break;
0003197:         }
0003198: 
0003199:         let class_name = if let Some(ref get_name) = get_class_name_fn {
0003200:             let name_ptr = get_name(current_class);
0003201:             if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0003202:                 let s = std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char);
0003203:                 s.to_string_lossy().to_string()
0003204:             } else {
0003205:                 format!("depth{}", depth)
0003206:             }
0003207:         } else {
0003208:             format!("depth{}", depth)
0003209:         };
0003210: 
0003211:         let mut iter: *mut c_void = ptr::null_mut();
0003212:         loop {
0003213:             if all_methods.len() >= max_methods {
0003214:                 break;
0003215:             }
0003216:             let method_info = get_methods_fn.unwrap()(current_class, &mut iter);
0003217:             if method_info.is_null() {
0003218:                 break;
0003219:             }
0003220: 
0003221:             let method_name = if let Some(ref get_name) = get_method_name_fn {
0003222:                 let name_ptr = get_name(method_info);
0003223:                 if !name_ptr.is_null() {
0003224:                     let s = std::ffi::CStr::from_ptr(name_ptr as *const c_char);
0003225:                     s.to_string_lossy().to_string()
0003226:                 } else {
0003227:                     String::from("?")
0003228:                 }
0003229:             } else {
0003230:                 String::from("?")
0003231:             };
0003232: 
0003233:             all_methods.push(format!(
0003234:                 r#"{{"name":"{}","class":"{}"}}"#,
0003235:                 method_name, class_name
0003236:             ));
0003237:         }
0003238: 
0003239:         if let Some(ref get_parent) = get_parent_fn {
0003240:             let parent = get_parent(current_class);
0003241:             if parent.is_null() || parent == current_class {
0003242:                 break;
0003243:             }
0003244:             current_class = parent;
0003245:         } else {
0003246:             break;
0003247:         }
0003248:         depth += 1;
0003249:     }
0003250: 
0003251:     format!(
0003252:         r#"{{"total":{},"methods":[{}]}}"#,
0003253:         all_methods.len(),
0003254:         all_methods.join(",")
0003255:     )
0003256: }

0003262: unsafe fn find_method_in_all_classes(method_name: &str) -> String {
0003263:     if API.is_null() {
0003264:         return r#"{"error":"api_null"}"#.to_string();
0003265:     }
0003266: 
0003267:     let image = match get_image() {
0003268:         img if !img.is_null() => img,
0003269:         _ => return r#"{"error":"image_null"}"#.to_string(),
0003270:     };
0003271: 
0003272:     let get_method_fn: Option<FnClassGetMethodFromName> = {
0003273:         let p = resolve_il2cpp_symbol("il2cpp_class_get_method_from_name");
0003274:         if p.is_null() {
0003275:             None
0003276:         } else {
0003277:             Some(std::mem::transmute::<*mut c_void, FnClassGetMethodFromName>(p))
0003278:         }
0003279:     };
0003280: 
0003281:     if get_method_fn.is_none() {
0003282:         return r#"{"error":"no_class_get_method_from_name"}"#.to_string();
0003283:     }
0003284: 
0003285:     let method_name_c = to_cstr(method_name);
0003286:     let mut found: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
0003287: 
0003288:     for (ns, cls) in KNOWN_CLASSES {
0003289:         let ns_c = to_cstr(ns);
0003290:         let cls_c = to_cstr(cls);
0003291:         let class = find_class(image, ns_c.as_ptr(), cls_c.as_ptr());
0003292:         if class.is_null() {
0003293:             continue;
0003294:         }
0003295: 
0003296:         let full_name = if ns.is_empty() {
0003297:             cls.to_string()
0003298:         } else {
0003299:             format!("{}.{}", ns, cls)
0003300:         };
0003301: 
0003302:         let method = get_method_fn.unwrap()(class, method_name_c.as_ptr(), 0);
0003303:         if !method.is_null() {
0003304:             found.push(format!(r#"{{"class":"{}","args":0}}"#, full_name));
0003305:         }
0003306: 
0003307:         let method1 = get_method_fn.unwrap()(class, method_name_c.as_ptr(), 1);
0003308:         if !method1.is_null() && method.is_null() {
0003309:             found.push(format!(r#"{{"class":"{}","args":1}}"#, full_name));
0003310:         }
0003311:     }
0003312: 
0003313:     format!(
0003314:         r#"{{"method":"{}","found":{},"results":[{}]}}"#,
0003315:         method_name,
0003316:         !found.is_empty(),
0003317:         found.join(",")
0003318:     )
0003319: }
